{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Full bit-width PPL profiler for quant-only MCKP.\\nProfiles each KV head at {2, 4, 8, 16}-bit quantization.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport math\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom eval_ppl import patch_attention, restore_attention\\n\\n\\nclass FullBitwidthProfiler:\\n    def __init__(self, model_path, calibration_data, device='cuda:0',\\n                 num_samples=16, seq_len=512):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.num_samples = num_samples\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Loading model {model_path}...\\\")\\n        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path)\\n        self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n        )\\n        self.model = self.model.to(device)\\n        self.model.eval()\\n\\n        config = self.model.config\\n        self.num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        self.num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\n\\n        self.input_ids = self._load_calibration_data(calibration_data)\\n        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads\\n        print(f\\\"Config: {self.num_layers} layers, {self.num_kv_heads} KV heads/layer, \\\"\\n              f\\\"{total_heads} heads total\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Calibration: {self.input_ids.shape[0]} seqs x {self.input_ids.shape[1]} tokens\\\")\\n\\n        self._baseline_ppl = None\\n\\n    def _load_calibration_data(self, path):\\n        with open(path) as f:\\n            samples = json.load(f)\\n        all_ids = []\\n        for s in samples:\\n            tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(s[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n            if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:\\n                all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])\\n            if len(all_ids) >= self.num_samples:\\n                break\\n        if not all_ids:\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"No calibration seqs with >= {self.seq_len} tokens\\\")\\n        return torch.tensor(all_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\\n\\n    def _compute_ppl(self):\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(\\n                self.input_ids, use_cache=False,\\n                cache_position=torch.arange(self.seq_len, device=self.device)\\n            )\\n        shift_logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1].contiguous()\\n        shift_labels = self.input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\"\\n        )\\n        total_tokens = shift_labels.numel()\\n        return math.exp(loss.item() / total_tokens)\\n\\n    def compute_baseline_ppl(self):\\n        if self._baseline_ppl is None:\\n            print(\\\"Computing baseline PPL...\\\")\\n            self._baseline_ppl = self._compute_ppl()\\n            print(f\\\"Baseline PPL: {self._baseline_ppl:.4f}\\\")\\n        return self._baseline_ppl\\n\\n    def profile_single_head(self, layer, head, bits):\\n        _ = self.compute_baseline_ppl()\\n        if bits >= 16:\\n            return 0.0\\n        allocations = {f\\\"{layer},{head}\\\": [\\\"quant\\\", bits]}\\n        orig_fwds = patch_attention(self.model, allocations)\\n        ppl = self._compute_ppl()\\n        restore_attention(self.model, orig_fwds)\\n        return ppl - self._baseline_ppl\\n\\n    def profile_all(self, bit_widths=(2, 4, 8)):\\n        _ = self.compute_baseline_ppl()\\n        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads\\n        total_evals = total_heads * len(bit_widths)\\n        done = 0\\n\\n        profiles = {}\\n        for l in range(self.num_layers):\\n            profiles[str(l)] = {}\\n            for h in range(self.num_kv_heads):\\n                head_profile = {\\\"quantization\\\": {}}\\n                for bits in bit_widths:\\n                    delta = self.profile_single_head(l, h, bits)\\n                    head_profile[\\\"quantization\\\"][str(bits)] = delta\\n                    done += 1\\n                    print(f\\\"[{done}/{total_evals}] L{l}H{h} @{bits}bit: delta={delta:.6f}\\\")\\n                profiles[str(l)][str(h)] = head_profile\\n\\n        return profiles\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"}

1e-12:\\n            pareto.append(a)\\n\\n    # Sort by memory descending for greedy downgrade\\n    pareto.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return pareto\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"full\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve MCKP for KV cache allocation via greedy downgrade.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        profiles: {layer: {head: {eviction: {ratio: mse}, quantization: {bits: mse}}}}\\n        budget: target average memory ratio per head (e.g. 0.25 for 4x compression)\\n        mode: \\\"full\\\" (eviction + quantization) or \\\"quant_only\\\"\\n    Returns:\\n        dict with allocation, total_distortion, actual_memory_ratio\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            pareto = _build_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode)\\n            heads.append((l, h, pareto))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n\\n    # Start all at most expensive (index 0 = full_16bit)\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n\\n    # Greedy downgrade: pick lowest efficiency (distortion_increase / memory_saved)\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] -       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"MCKP solver for KV cache allocation.\\nInput: distortion profiles + memory budget\\nOutput: per-head allocation decisions\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\n\\n\\ndef _build_pareto(head_data, mode):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build Pareto-optimal action set for a single head.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    actions = [(1.0, 0.0, \\\"full\\\", 16)]\\n\\n    if mode == \\\"full\\\":\\n        for r_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"eviction\\\", {}).items():\\n            r = float(r_str)\\n            actions.append((r, mse, \\\"evict\\\", r))\\n\\n    for b_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"quantization\\\", {}).items():\\n        b = int(float(b_str))\\n        actions.append((b / 16.0, mse, \\\"quant\\\", b))\\n\\n    # Deduplicate by memory: keep lowest distortion per memory level\\n    by_mem = {}\\n    for a in actions:\\n        mem = round(a[0], 6)\\n        if mem not in by_mem or a[1] < by_mem[mem][1]:\\n            by_mem[mem] = a\\n\\n    # Sort by memory ascending, build Pareto front\\n    sorted_actions = sorted(by_mem.values(), key=lambda x: x[0])\\n    pareto = [sorted_actions[0]]\\n    for a in sorted_actions[1:]:\\n        if a[1] < pareto[-1][1] - 1e-12:\\n            pareto.append(a)\\n\\n    # Sort by memory descending for greedy downgrade\\n    pareto.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return pareto\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"full\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve MCKP for KV cache allocation via greedy downgrade.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        profiles: {layer: {head: {eviction: {ratio: mse}, quantization: {bits: mse}}}}\\n        budget: target average memory ratio per head (e.g. 0.25 for 4x compression)\\n        mode: \\\"full\\\" (eviction + quantization) or \\\"quant_only\\\"\\n    Returns:\\n        dict with allocation, total_distortion, actual_memory_ratio\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            pareto = _build_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode)\\n            heads.append((l, h, pareto))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n\\n    # Start all at most expensive (index 0 = full_16bit)\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n\\n    # Greedy downgrade: pick lowest efficiency (distortion_increase / memory_saved)\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - nxt[0]\\n                if mem_saved < 1e-12:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"

\\\"\\\"\\\"Monkey-patch attention layers to apply per-head compression.\\n\\n    Returns dict of original forward methods for restoration.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    config = model.config\\n     v = orig_attn.v_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n\\n                cos, sin = position_embeddings\\n                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)\\n\\n                # Per-head quantization\\n                for h in range(n_kv):\\n                    atype, param = h_allocs[h]\\n                    if atype == \\\"quant\\\" and int(param) < 16:\\n                        bits = int(param)\\n                        k[:, h] = quantize_per_channel(k[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)\\n                        v[:, h] = quantize_per_token(v[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)\\n\\n                k_exp = repeat_kv(k, ng)\\n                v_exp = repeat_kv(v, ng)\\n\\n                attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, restore_attention(model, original_forwards):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Restore original attention forwards.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():\\n        model.model.layers[layer_idx].self_attn.forward = fwd\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device, stride=512):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate perplexity on WikiText-2.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-2-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"test\\\")\\n    text = \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\".join([t for t in ds[\\\"text\\\"] if t.strip()])\\n\\n    encodings = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")\\n    input_ids = encodings.input_ids.to(device)\\n    total_len = input_ids.shape[1]\\n\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    total_tokens = 0\\n    n_chunks = (total_len - 1) // stride\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Evaluating perplexity on {total_len} tokens, stride={stride}, {n_chunks} chunks\\\")\\n\\n    for i, begin in shift_labels = chunk[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\",\\n        )\\n        total_loss += loss.item()\\n        total_tokens += shift_labels.numel()\\n\\n        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\\n            print(f\\\"  chunk {i + 1}/{n_chunks}, running ppl={math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens):.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    ppl = math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model\\\", type=str, default=\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--allocation\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", type=str, default=\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--stride\\\", type=int, default=512)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    with AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n    )\\n    model = model.to(args.device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    print(\\\"Patching attention layers...\\\")\\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, allocations)\\n\\n    ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, emask[top_idx] = 0\\n                            attn_scores[:, qs:qe] = attn_scores[:, qs:qe] + emask\\n\\n                attn_w = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(q.dtype)\\n                out = torch.matmul(attn_w, v_exp)\\n                out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().reshape(bsz, seq_len, -1)\\n                out = orig_attn.o_proj(out)\\n                return out, attn_w\\n\\n            return compressed_forward\\n\\n        attn.forward = make_forward(attn, head_allocs, has_eviction)\\n\\n    return original_forwards\\n\\n\\ndef restore_attention(model, original_forwards):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Restore original attention forwards.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():\\n        model.model.layers[layer_idx].self_attn.forward = fwd\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device, stride=512):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate perplexity on WikiText-2.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-2-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"test\\\")\\n    text = \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\".join([t for t in ds[\\\"text\\\"] if t.strip()])\\n\\n    encodings = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")\\n    input_ids = encodings.input_ids.to(device)\\n    total_len = input_ids.shape[1]\\n\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    total_tokens = 0\\n    n_chunks = (total_len - 1) // stride\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Evaluating perplexity on {total_len} tokens, stride={stride}, {n_chunks} chunks\\\")\\n\\n    for i, begin in enumerate(range(0, total_len - 1, stride)):\\n        end = min(begin + stride, total_len)\\n        chunk = input_ids[:, begin:end]\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(chunk, use_cache=False, cache_position=torch.arange(chunk.shape[1], device=chunk.device))\\n            logits = outputs.logits\\n\\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1].contiguous()\\n        shift_labels = chunk[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\",\\n        )\\n        total_loss += loss.item()\\n        total_tokens += shift_labels.numel()\\n\\n        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\\n            print(f\\\"  chunk {i + 1}/{n_chunks}, running ppl={math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens):.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    ppl = math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model\\\", type=str, default=\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--allocation\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", type=str, default=\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--stride\\\", type=int, default=512)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    with open(args.allocation) as f:\\n        alloc_data = json.load(f)\\n    allocations = alloc_data[\\\"allocation\\\"]\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading model {args.model}...\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n    )\\n    model = model.to(args.device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    print(\\\"Patching attention layers...\\\")\\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, allocations)\\n\\n    ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, args.device, args.stride)\\n    print(f\\\"Perplexity: {ppl:.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def solve_mckp_constrained(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\",\\n                           max_2bit=8, l0_exclude_2bit=True):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MCKP with cardinality constraints (D009).\\n    - max_2bit: at most K heads at 2-bit (K=8, manual experiment safe zone)\\n    - l0_exclude_2bit: Layer 0 heads excluded from 2-bit (cliff effect)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            exclude = {2} if (l0_exclude_2bit and l == 0) else set()\\n            actions = _build_actions_no_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode, exclude)\\n            heads.append((l, h, actions))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n    n_2bit = 0\\n\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_saved = 0.0\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - nxt[0]\\n                if mem_saved < 1e-12:\\n                    continue\\n                # D009: cardinality constraint on 2-bit\\n                is_2bit = (nxt[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and nxt[3] == 2)\\n                if is_2bit and max_2bit is not None and n_2bit >= max_2bit:\\n                    continue\\n                eff = (nxt[1] - curr[1]) / mem_saved\\n                # Prefer lower eff; break ties by larger mem_saved for faster convergence\\n                if eff < best_eff - 1e-12 or (abs(eff - best_eff) < 1e-12 and mem_saved > best_saved):\\n                    best_eff = eff\\n                    best_saved = mem_saved\\n                    best_key = (l, h)\\n                    best_idx = ni\\n\\n        if best_key is None:\\n            break\\n\\n        l, h = best_key\\n        for ll, hh, actions in heads:\\n            if ll == l and hh == h:\\n                old = actions[current[best_key]]\\n                new = actions[best_idx]\\n                total_memory -= (old[0] - new[0])\\n                was_2bit = (old[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and old[3] == 2)\\n                is_now_2bit = (new[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and new[3] == 2)\\n                if is_now_2bit and not was_2bit:\\n                    n_2bit += 1\\n                elif was_2bit and not is_now_2bit:\\n                    n_2bit -= 1\\n                current[best_key] = best_idx\\n                break\\n\\n    allocation = {}\\n    total_dist = 0.0\\n    actual_mem = 0.0\\n    bit_dist = {}\\n    for l, h, actions in heads:\\n        a = actions[current[(l, h)]]\\n        allocation[f\\\"{l},{h}\\\"] = [a[2], a[3]]\\n        total_dist += a[1]\\n        actual_mem += a[0]\\n        b = a[3]\\n        bit_dist[b] = bit_dist.get(b, 0) + 1\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"allocation\\\": allocation,\\n        \\\"total_distortion\\\": total_dist,\\n        \\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\": actual_mem / n_heads,\\n        \\\"bit_distribution\\\": bit_dist,\\n        \\\"n_2bit\\\": n_2bit,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef solve_greedy_ratequant(profiles, budget=0.25, bit_options=None,\\n                           mode=\\\"quant_only\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"RateQuant-style greedy on discrete {2,4,8,16} bit-widths.\\n    Start all heads at max bit-width, greedily downgrade head with\\n    smallest marginal distortion/bit gain until budget met.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if bit_options is None:\\n        bit_options = [2, 4, 8, 16]\\n    bit_options = sorted(bit_options)\\n\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            qdata = profiles[l_str][h_str].get(\\\"quantization\\\", {})\\n            distortion_map = {}\\n            for b in bit_options:\\n                if b == 16:\\n                    distortion_map[16] = 0.0\\n                else:\\n                    b_str = str(b)\\n                    if b_str in qdata:\\n                        distortion_map[b] = qdata[b_str]\\n                    elif str(float(b)) in qdata:\\n                        distortion_map[b] = qdata[str(float(b))]\\n            heads.append((l, h, distortion_map))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    allocation = [max(bit_options)] * n_heads\\n    target_mem = budget * n_heads * max(bit_options)\\n\\n    def current_mem():\\n        return sum(allocation)\\n\\n    while current_mem() > target_mem + 1e-9:\\n        best_head = None\\n        best_gain = float('inf')\\n\\n        for i in range(n_heads):\\n            cur_bits = allocation[i]\\n            lower = [b for b in bit_options if b < cur_bits]\\n            if not lower:\\n                continue\\n            next_lower = max(lower)\\n\\n            cur_dist = heads[i][2].get(cur_bits, 0.0)\\n            nxt_dist = heads[i][2].get(next_lower, None)\\n            if nxt_dist is None:\\n                continue\\n\\n            bits_saved = cur_bits - next_lower\\n            dist_inc = nxt_dist - cur_dist\\n            marginal = dist_inc / bits_saved\\n\\n            if marginal < best_gain:\\n                best_gain = marginal\\n                best_head = i\\n\\n        if best_head is None:\\n            break\\n\\n        cur = allocation[best_head]\\n        lower = [b for b in bit_options if b < cur]\\n        allocation[best_head] = max(lower)\\n\\n    alloc_dict = {}\\n    total_dist = 0.0\\n    bit_dist = {}\\n    for i, (l, h, dmap) in enumerate(heads):\\n        b = allocation[i]\\n        alloc_dict[f\\\"{l},{h}\\\"] = [\\\"quant\\\" if b < 16 else \\\"full\\\", b]\\n        total_dist += dmap.get(b, 0.0)\\n        bit_dist[b] = bit_dist.get(b, 0) + 1\\n\\n    actual_ratio = sum(allocation) / (n_heads * max(bit_options))\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"allocation\\\": alloc_dict,\\n        \\\"total_distortion\\\": total_dist,\\n        \\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\": actual_ratio,\\n        \\\"bit_distribution\\\": bit_dist,\\n        \\\"n_2bit\\\": bit_dist.get(2, 0),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef enforce_monotonicity(profiles):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Enforce delta(lower_bits) >= delta(higher_bits) via isotonic regression.\\n    Handles calibration noise where e.g. delta_4bit > delta_2bit.\\n    Returns a new profiles dict (original unchanged).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    import copy\\n    enforced = copy.deepcopy(profiles)\\n    bit_order = [16, 8, 4, 2]  # high to low\\n\\n    for l_str in enforced:\\n        for h_str in enforced[l_str]:\\n            qdata = enforced[l_str][h_str].get(\\\"quantization\\\", {})\\n            if not qdata:\\n                continue\\n            # Collect deltas in descending bit order\\n            deltas = {}\\n            for b in bit_order:\\n                key = str(b)\\n                if key in qdata:\\n                    deltas[b] = qdata[key]\\n                elif b == 16:\\n                    deltas[16] = 0.0\\n\\n            # Isotonic enforcement: scan from high to low bits\\n            # delta[b] = max(delta[b], delta[b_higher])\\n            prev = deltas.get(16, 0.0)\\n            for b in bit_order:\\n                if b not in deltas:\\n                    continue\\n                if deltas[b] < prev:\\n                    deltas[b] = prev\\n                prev = deltas[b]\\n                if b != 16:\\n                    qdata[str(b)] = deltas[b]\\n\\n    return enforced\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}

FP16 baseline (no compression)\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\"*60)\\n    print(\\\"FP16 BASELINE (no compression)\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\"*60)\\n    fp16_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\\n    print(f\\\">>> FP16 PPL = {fp16_ppl:.4f}\\\")\\n\\n    results = []\\n    for cfg in BUDGETS:\\n        budget = cfg[\\\"budget\\\"]\\n        comp = cfg[\\\"compression\\\"]\\n        ubits = cfg[\\\"uniform_bits\\\"]\\n        max_2bit = cfg[\\\"max_2bit\\\"]\\n\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"BUDGET = {budget} ({comp} compression)\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"{'='*60}\\\")\\n\\n        # --- Uniform baseline ---\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n--- Uniform {ubits}-bit baseline ---\\\")\\n        uniform_sol = uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=ubits)\\n        orig = patch_attention(model, uniform_sol[\\\"allocation\\\"])\\n        uniform_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\\n        restore_attention(model, orig)\\n        print(f\\\">>> Uniform {ubits}-bit PPL = {uniform_ppl:.4f}\\\")\\n\\n        # --- Constrained MCKP ---\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n--- Constrained MCKP (max_2bit={max_2bit}) ---\\\")\\n        t0 = time.time()\\n        mckp_sol = solve_mckp_constrained(\\n            profiles, budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\",\\n            max_2bit=max_2bit, l0_exclude_2bit=True\\n        )\\n        t_solve = time.time() - t0\\n\\n        bit_dist = mckp_sol[\\\"bit_distribution\\\"]\\n        mem_ratio = mckp_sol[\\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\"]\\n        print(f\\\"Bit dist: {bit_dist}, mem_ratio: {mem_ratio:.4f}, solve: {t_solve:.2f}s\\\")\\n\\n        orig = patch_attention(model, mckp_sol[\\\"allocation\\\"])\\n        mckp_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\\n        restore_attention(model, orig)\\n        print(f\\\">>> MCKP PPL = {mckp_ppl:.4f}\\\")\\n\\n        delta = uniform_ppl - mckp_ppl\\n        print(f\\\">>> Delta (uniform - mckp) = {delta:.4f}\\\")\\n\\n        results.append({\\n            \\\"budget\\\": budget,\\n            \\\"compression\\\": comp,\\n            \\\"uniform_bits\\\": ubits,\\n            \\\"uniform_ppl\\\": round(uniform_ppl, 4),\\n            \\\"mckp_ppl\\\": round(mckp_ppl, 4),\\n            \\\"delta_ppl\\\": round(delta, 4),\\n            \\\"mckp_mem_ratio\\\": round(mem_ratio, 4),\\n            \\\"bit_distribution\\\": bit_dist,\\n            \\\"n_2bit\\\": bit_dist.get(2, 0),\\n            \\\"n_4bit\\\": bit_dist.get(4, 0),\\n            \\\"n_8bit\\\": bit_dist.get(8, 0),\\n            \\\"n_16bit\\\": bit_dist.get(16, 0),\\n            \\\"max_2bit_constraint\\\": max_2bit,\\n            \\\"total_distortion\\\": round(mckp_sol[\\\"total_distortion\\\"], 6),\\n            \\\"mckp_allocation\\\": mckp_sol[\\\"allocation\\\"],\\n        })\\n\\n    # Save JSON\\n    output = {\\n        \\\"fp16_ppl\\\": round(fp16_ppl, 4),\\n        \\\"results\\\": results,\\n    }\\n    with open(\\\"budget_sweep_results.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\\n\\n    # Save CSV\\n    with open(\\\"budget_sweep_results.csv\\\", \\\"w\\\", newline=\\\"\\\") as f:\\n        w = csv.writer(f)\\n        w.writerow([\\\"compression\\\", \\\"budget\\\", \\\"uniform_bits\\\", \\\"uniform_ppl\\\",\\n                     \\\"mckp_ppl\\\", \\\"delta_ppl\\\", \\\"mckp_mem_ratio\\\",\\n                     \\\"n_2bit\\\", \\\"n_4bit\\\", \\\"n_8bit\\\", \\\"n_16bit\\\"])\\n        for r in results:\\n            w.writerow([r[\\\"compression\\\"], r[\\\"budget\\\"], r[\\\"uniform_bits\\\"],\\n                        r[\\\"uniform_ppl\\\"], r[\\\"mckp_ppl\\\"], r[\\\"delta_ppl\\\"],\\n                        r[\\\"mckp_mem_ratio\\\"],\\n                        r[\\\"n_2bit\\\"], r[\\\"n_4bit\\\"], r[\\\"n_8bit\\\"], r[\\\"n_16bit\\\"]])\\n\\n    #   "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/run_c4_cross_dataset.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"C4 Cross-Dataset Evaluation (plan_007).\nProfile with C4 calibration data, solve MCKP, eval PPL, compare with WikiText-2 results.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport time\nimport torch\nfrom full_bitwidth_profiler import FullBitwidthProfiler\nfrom solver import solve_mckp_constrained, uniform_quantization_baseline, enforce_monotonicity\nfrom eval_ppl import patch_attention, restore_attention, evaluate_perplexity\n\nMODEL = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-7B\"\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nC4_CALIBRATION = \"c4_calibration.json\"\nWT2_REF_FILE = \"budget_sweep_results.json\"\nBUDGET = 0.25\nMAX_2BIT = 8\n\n\ndef load_wt2_reference():\n    with open(WT2_REF_FILE) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    for r in data[\"results\"]:\n        if abs(r[\"budget\"] - BUDGET) < 1e-6:\n            return r\n    raise ValueError(f\"No budget={BUDGET} entry in {WT2_REF_FILE}\")\n\n\ndef compute_agreement(alloc_a, alloc_b):\n    keys = set(alloc_a.keys()) & set(alloc_b.keys())\n    if not keys:\n        return 0.0, {}\n    agree = 0\n    disagree_details = {}\n    for k in sorted(keys):\n        a_type, a_param = alloc_a[k]\n        b_type, b_param = alloc_b[k]\n        if a_type == b_type and a_param == b_param:\n            agree += 1\n        else:\n            disagree_details[k] = {\"wt2\": [a_type, a_param], \"c4\": [b_type, b_param]}\n    return agree / len(keys), disagree_details\n\n\ndef main():\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # === Phase 1: C4 Profiling ===\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"[Phase 1] Profiling with C4 calibration data...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    profiler = FullBitwidthProfiler(\n        model_path=MODEL,\n        calibration_data=C4_CALIBRATION,\n        device=DEVICE,\n        num_samples=16,\n        seq_len=512\n    )\n    baseline_cal_ppl = profiler.compute_baseline_ppl()\n    raw_profiles = profiler.profile_all(bit_widths=(2, 4, 8))\n    profile_time = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"\\nC4 profiling done in {profile_time:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Save raw profiles\n    profiling_output = {\n        \"calibration_source\": \"c4\",\n        \"baseline_calibration_ppl\": baseline_cal_ppl,\n        \"profile_time_seconds\": profile_time,\n        \"profiles\": raw_profiles,\n    }\n    with open(\"c4_profiling_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(profiling_output, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved c4_profiling_results.json\")\n\n    # === Phase 2: Enforce monotonicity + Solve MCKP ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"[Phase 2] Solving constrained MCKP (budget=0.25, max_2bit=8)...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    profiles = enforce_monotonicity(raw_profiles)\n    mckp_sol = solve_mckp_constrained(\n        profiles, BUDGET, mode=\"quant_only\",\n        max_2bit=MAX_2BIT, l0_exclude_2bit=True\n    )\n    uniform_sol = uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4)\n\n    print(f\"MCKP bit distribution: {mckp_sol['bit_distribution']}\")\n    print(f\"MCKP memory ratio: {mckp_sol['actual_memory_ratio']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"MCKP total distortion: {mckp_sol['total_distortion']:.6f}\")\n\n    # === Phase 3: WikiText-2 PPL Eval ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"[Phase 3] WikiText-2 PPL evaluation...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    model = profiler.model\n    tokenizer = profiler.tokenizer\n    t1 = time.time()\n\n    print(\"\\n  Evaluating FP16 baseline...\")\n    fp16_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\n    print(f\"  >>> FP16 PPL = {fp16_ppl:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n  Evaluating C4-calibrated MCKP allocation...\")\n    orig = patch_attention(model, mckp_sol[\"allocation\"])\n    c4_mckp_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\n    restore_attention(model, orig)\n    print(f\"  >>> C4-MCKP PPL = {c4_mckp_ppl:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n  Evaluating uniform 4-bit...\")\n    orig = patch_attention(model, uniform_sol[\"allocation\"])\n    uniform_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\n    restore_attention(model, orig)\n    print(f\"  >>> Uniform 4-bit PPL = {uniform_ppl:.4f}\")\n\n    eval_time = time.time() - t1\n\n    # === Phase 4: Cross-dataset comparison ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"[Phase 4] Cross-dataset comparison...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    wt2_ref = load_wt2_reference()\n    wt2_mckp_ppl = wt2_ref[\"mckp_ppl\"]\n    wt2_uniform_ppl = wt2_ref[\"uniform_ppl\"]\n    wt2_alloc = wt2_ref[\"mckp_allocation\"]\n    wt2_bit_dist = wt2_ref[\"bit_distribution\"]\n\n    agreement_rate, disagree_details = compute_agreement(wt2_alloc, mckp_sol[\"allocation\"])\n\n    c4_delta = uniform_ppl - c4_mckp_ppl\n    wt2_delta = wt2_uniform_ppl - wt2_mckp_ppl\n\n    total_time = time.time() - t_start\n\n    # === Print summary ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 70}\")\n    print(\"CROSS-DATASET COMPARISON RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(f\"\\n{'Metric':<40} {'WikiText-2 cal':>15} {'C4 cal':>15}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 70)\n    print(f\"{'Calibration baseline PPL':<40} {'N/A':>15} {baseline_cal_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'FP16 WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {fp16_ppl:>15.4f} {fp16_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'MCKP WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {wt2_mckp_ppl:>15.4f} {c4_mckp_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'Uniform 4-bit WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {wt2_uniform_ppl:>15.4f} {uniform_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'Delta (uniform - MCKP)':<40} {wt2_delta:>+15.4f} {c4_delta:>+15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Bit distribution (WT2 cal):':<40} {wt2_bit_dist}\")\n    print(f\"{'Bit distribution (C4 cal):':<40} {mckp_sol['bit_distribution']}\")\n    print(f\"\\nAllocation agreement rate: {agreement_rate:.1%} ({int(agreement_rate * 112)}/112 heads)\")\n    print(f\"Number of disagreements: {len(disagree_details)}\")\n\n    if disagree_details:\n        print(\"\\nDisagreement details (first 20):\")\n        for i, (k, v) in enumerate(sorted(disagree_details.items())):\n            if i >= 20:\n                print(f\"  ... and {len(disagree_details) - 20} more\")\n                break\n            print(f\"  {k}: WT2={v['wt2']}, C4={v['c4']}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nProfile time: {profile_time:.1f}s, Eval time: {eval_time:.1f}s, Total: {total_time:.1f}s\")\n\n    # === Save results ===\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"c4_cross_dataset_eval\",\n        \"calibration_source\": \"c4\",\n        \"budget\": BUDGET,\n        \"max_2bit\": MAX_2BIT,\n        \"c4_calibration_baseline_ppl\": baseline_cal_ppl,\n        \"fp16_wikitext2_ppl\": fp16_ppl,\n        \"c4_mckp_wikitext2_ppl\": c4_mckp_ppl,\n        \"uniform4bit_wikitext2_ppl\": uniform_ppl,\n        \"c4_mckp_delta_over_uniform\": c4_delta,\n        \"c4_mckp_bit_distribution\": mckp_sol[\"bit_distribution\"],\n        \"c4_mckp_memory_ratio\": mckp_sol[\"actual_memory_ratio\"],\n        \"c4_mckp_total_distortion\": mckp_sol[\"total_distortion\"],\n        \"c4_mckp_allocation\": mckp_sol[\"allocation\"],\n        \"wt2_reference\": {\n            \"mckp_ppl\": wt2_mckp_ppl,\n            \"uniform_ppl\": wt2_uniform_ppl,\n            \"delta\": wt2_delta,\n            \"bit_distribution\": wt2_bit_dist,\n        },\n        \"cross_dataset_comparison\": {\n            \"allocation_agreement_rate\": agreement_rate,\n            \"n_agree\": int(agreement_rate * 112),\n            \"n_disagree\": len(disagree_details),\n            \"disagree_details\": disagree_details,\n            \"ppl_gap_mckp\": c4_mckp_ppl - wt2_mckp_ppl,\n            \"delta_gap\": c4_delta - wt2_delta,\n        },\n        \"timing\": {\n            \"profile_seconds\": profile_time,\n            \"eval_seconds\": eval_time,\n            \"total_seconds\": total_time,\n        },\n        \"c4_profiles\": raw_profiles,\n    }\n\n    with open(\"c4_eval_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"\\nSaved c4_eval_results.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

1	"""C4 Cross-Dataset Evaluation (plan_007).
2	Profile with C4 calibration data, solve MCKP, eval PPL, compare with WikiText-2 results.
3	"""
4	import json
5	import time
6	import torch
7	from full_bitwidth_profiler import FullBitwidthProfiler
8	from solver import solve_mckp_constrained, uniform_quantization_baseline, enforce_monotonicity
9	from eval_ppl import patch_attention, restore_attention, evaluate_perplexity
10	
11	MODEL = "/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-7B"
12	DEVICE = "cuda:0"
13	C4_CALIBRATION = "c4_calibration.json"
14	WT2_REF_FILE = "budget_sweep_results.json"
15	BUDGET = 0.25
16	MAX_2BIT = 8
17	
18	
19	def load_wt2_reference():
20	    with open(WT2_REF_FILE) as f:
21	        data = json.load(f)
22	    for r in data["results"]:
23	        if abs(r["budget"] - BUDGET) < 1e-6:
24	            return r
25	    raise ValueError(f"No budget={BUDGET} entry in {WT2_REF_FILE}")
26	
27	
28	def compute_agreement(alloc_a, alloc_b):
29	    keys = set(alloc_a.keys()) & set(alloc_b.keys())
30	    if not keys:
31	        return 0.0, {}
32	    agree = 0
33	    disagree_details = {}
34	    for k in sorted(keys):
35	        a_type, a_param = alloc_a[k]
36	        b_type, b_param = alloc_b[k]
37	        if a_type == b_type and a_param == b_param:
38	            agree += 1
39	        else:
40	            disagree_details[k] = {"wt2": [a_type, a_param], "c4": [b_type, b_param]}
41	    return agree / len(keys), disagree_details
42	
43	
44	def main():
45	    t_start = time.time()
46	
47	    # === Phase 1: C4 Profiling ===
48	    print("=" * 60)
49	    print("[Phase 1] Profiling with C4 calibration data...")
50	    print("=" * 60)
51	    t0 = time.time()
52	
53	    profiler = FullBitwidthProfiler(
54	        model_path=MODEL,
55	        calibration_data=C4_CALIBRATION,
56	        device=DEVICE,
57	        num_samples=16,
58	        seq_len=512
59	    )
60	    baseline_cal_ppl = profiler.compute_baseline_ppl()
61	    raw_profiles = profiler.profile_all(bit_widths=(2, 4, 8))
62	    profile_time = time.time() - t0
63	    print(f"\nC4 profiling done in {profile_time:.1f}s")
64	
65	    # Save raw profiles
66	    profiling_output = {
67	        "calibration_source": "c4",
68	        "baseline_calibration_ppl": baseline_cal_ppl,
69	        "profile_time_seconds": profile_time,
70	        "profiles": raw_profiles,
71	    }
72	    with open("c4_profiling_results.json", "w") as f:
73	        json.dump(profiling_output, f, indent=2)
74	    print("Saved c4_profiling_results.json")
75	
76	    # === Phase 2: Enforce monotonicity + Solve MCKP ===
77	    print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
78	    print("[Phase 2] Solving constrained MCKP (budget=0.25, max_2bit=8)...")
79	    print("=" * 60)
80	
81	    profiles = enforce_monotonicity(raw_profiles)
82	    mckp_sol = solve_mckp_constrained(
83	        profiles, BUDGET, mode="quant_only",
84	        max_2bit=MAX_2BIT, l0_exclude_2bit=True
85	    )
86	    uniform_sol = uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4)
87	
88	    print(f"MCKP bit distribution: {mckp_sol['bit_distribution']}")
89	    print(f"MCKP memory ratio: {mckp_sol['actual_memory_ratio']:.4f}")
90	    print(f"MCKP total distortion: {mckp_sol['total_distortion']:.6f}")
91	
92	    # === Phase 3: WikiText-2 PPL Eval ===
93	    print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
94	    print("[Phase 3] WikiText-2 PPL evaluation...")
95	    print("=" * 60)
96	
97	    model = profiler.model
98	    tokenizer = profiler.tokenizer
99	    t1 = time.time()
100	
101	    print("\n  Evaluating FP16 baseline...")
102	    fp16_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)
103	    print(f"  >>> FP16 PPL = {fp16_ppl:.4f}")
104	
105	    print("\n  Evaluating C4-calibrated MCKP allocation...")
106	    orig = patch_attention(model, mckp_sol["allocation"])
107	    c4_mckp_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)
108	    restore_attention(model, orig)
109	    print(f"  >>> C4-MCKP PPL = {c4_mckp_ppl:.4f}")
110	
111	    print("\n  Evaluating uniform 4-bit...")
112	    orig = patch_attention(model, uniform_sol["allocation"])
113	    uniform_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)
114	    restore_attention(model, orig)
115	    print(f"  >>> Uniform 4-bit PPL = {uniform_ppl:.4f}")
116	
117	    eval_time = time.time() - t1
118	
119	    # === Phase 4: Cross-dataset comparison ===
120	    print(f"\n{'=' * 60}")
121	    print("[Phase 4] Cross-dataset comparison...")
122	    print("=" * 60)
123	
124	    wt2_ref = load_wt2_reference()
125	    wt2_mckp_ppl = wt2_ref["mckp_ppl"]
126	    wt2_uniform_ppl = wt2_ref["uniform_ppl"]
127	    wt2_alloc = wt2_ref["mckp_allocation"]
128	    wt2_bit_dist = wt2_ref["bit_distribution"]
129

131

133	    wt2_delta = wt2_uniform_ppl - wt2_mckp_ppl
134	
135	    total_time = time.time() - t_start
136

139	    print("CROSS-DATASET COMPARISON RESULTS")
140	    print("=" * 70)
141	    print(f"\n{'Metric':<40} {'WikiText-2 cal':>15} {'C4 cal':>15}")
142	    print("-" * 70)
143	    print(f"{'Calibration baseline PPL':<40} {'N/A':>15} {baseline_cal_ppl:>15.4f}")
144	    print(f"{'FP16 WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {fp16_ppl:>15.4f} {fp16_ppl:>15.4f}")
145	    print(f"{'MCKP WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {wt2_mckp_ppl:>15.4f} {c4_mckp_ppl:>15.4f}")
146	    print(f"{'Uniform 4-bit WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {wt2_uniform_ppl:>15.4f} {uniform_ppl:>15.4f}")
147	    print(f"{'Delta (uniform - MCKP)':<40} {wt2_delta:>+15.4f} {c4_delta:>+15.4f}")

149	    print(f"{'Bit distribution (C4 cal):':<40} {mckp_sol['bit_distribution']}")
150	    print(f"\nAllocation agreement rate: {agreement_rate:.1%} ({int(agreement_rate * 112)}/112 heads)")
151	    print(f"Number of disagreements: {len(disagree_details)}")
152	
153	    if disagree_details:
154	        print("\nDisagreement details (first 20):")
155	        for i, (k, v) in enumerate(sorted(disagree_details.items())):
156	            if i >= 20:
157	                print(f"  ... and {len(disagree_details) - 20} more")
158	                break
159	            print(f"  {k}: WT2={v['wt2']}, C4={v['c4']}")

Save raw profiles\n    profiling_output = {\n        \"calibration_source\": \"c4\",\n        \"baseline_calibration_ppl\": baseline_cal_ppl,\n        \"profile_time_seconds\": profile_time,\n        \"profiles\": raw_profiles,\n    }\n    with open(\"c4_profiling_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(profiling_output, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved c4_profiling_results.json\")\n\n    # Profiling is progressing. 218/336 done, so about 65% complete. But I notice all the deltas are 0.000000 which is suspicious. Wait - let me think about this. The profiler is using C4 calibration data and computing PPL on the calibration set itself. If the deltas are all 0, that's a problem.

Actually wait, let me re-read the profiler code. `profile_single_head` computes:
1. Baseline PPL on calibration data
2. Patches one head with quantization


3. Computes PPL on the patched model
4. Returns delta = patched_ppl - baseline_ppl

If all deltas are showing as 0.000000, that's definitely suspicious - either the values are so small they're rounding to zero, or there's a bug. And it's odd that WikiText-2 profiling also showed mckp_total_distortion of 0.0.

Looking back at the results, the unconstrained solver gave 0.0 distortion through Pareto filtering, while the constrained version produced 0.170428. But I need to check whether those profile deltas are actually zero or just displaying that way. Let me examine the actual values more carefully to see if this is a real issue or just a precision problem. me re-read the full_bitwidth_results.json to see what the WikiText-2 profiling deltas looked like.

Actually, the earlier output showed:
```
"mckp_total_distortion": 0.0,
```

But this was from the unconstrained solver on the full_bitwidth_results.json which showed verdict=FAIL. The budget_sweep used enforce_monotonicity on those same profiles and got distortion=0.170428.

Looking at the actual profiling deltas: In the run_full_bitwidth.py output, it printed sensitivity analysis with min/max/mean/std values. The fact that it computed reasonable results suggests the profiles weren't all zero.

But now the C4 profiling is showing all zeros. This could mean:
1. The quantization simulation in patch_attention has no effect with bfloat16 (unlikely, since it worked for WikiText-2)
2. There's a Python buffering issue causing truncated float formatting
3. The C4 calibration data has some issue

Let me check what the actual profile numbers look like, not just the printed delta. The printed format is `delta=0.000000` which shows 6 decimal places. If the actual delta is very small (< 0.0000005), it would show as 0.000000.

But from the WikiText-2 run, the 2-bit delta stats showed min=0.0001 range, with most being in the 0.001+ range. So deltas showing as exactly 0.000000 for many heads would be unusual. I should look more carefully at the full log output to see if some heads actually do have non-zero values, since I only saw a snippet earlier.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I can see the C4 profiling results. The C4 baseline PPL is 12.4354 (vs WikiText-2's 10.8011). 

Most heads have delta=0 but some significant heads have non-zero deltas:
- L0H0 @2bit: 4.047901 (most sensitive)
- L0H2 @2bit: 2.570062
- L0H3 @2bit: 1.224722
- L0H1 @4bit: 1.012530


- L0H1 @2bit: 0.803634
- L4H1 @2bit: 0.803634
- L0H2 @4bit: 0.395527

I'm noticing that many heads cluster around 0.196215 at lower bit widths, which looks like the minimum quantization noise floor. The pattern mirrors what I saw with WikiText-2—early layer heads are far more sensitive to quantization, while later layers stay stable at 4/8 bit with only minor effects at 2-bit. The profiling is about 65% complete and the results are tracking consistently so far.

That repeated 0.196215 value is striking—likely a quantization artifact from bfloat16 rounding rather than actual sensitivity, but since it's not zero the solver should still handle it. The run is progressing well at 218/336 samples, so I should see completion in another 15-20 minutes.
```

Since the budget_sweep_results.json already ran evaluate_perplexity successfully, WikiText-2 should be cached locally. The problem is that `load_dataset` tries to verify the online version even when a cached copy exists. I need to either enable the network proxy or set the offline mode flag, then restart with the proper environment configuration.
````

_source line 160_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
WikiText-2 is cached at `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/wikitext/wikitext-2-raw-v1`. But `load_dataset` by default uses `~/.cache/huggingface/datasets/` as cache dir unless `HF_HOME` or `HF_DATASETS_CACHE` is set.

The issue is that `load_dataset("wikitext", "wikitext-2-raw-v1", split="test")` tries to check the Hub even when cached at the default location. But the cached dataset is at `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/`, not the default `~/.cache/huggingface/datasets/`.


I need to set the environment variables `HF_HOME` or `HF_DATASETS_CACHE` to point to the actual cache location, and also set `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` to skip Hub checks entirely. Then I'll kill the current process and check if the profiling results were saved, so I can write a script that skips Phase 1 and just loads the cached profiles for the solver and evaluation steps.
```

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute · `toolu_01MXMfKmMCLb9BjnNptYCa3h`</summary>

```json
{
  "server": "westd-37397",
  "command": "cat > /root/ratekv/run_c4_phase2_4.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"C4 Cross-Dataset Eval: Phase 2-4 (solver + PPL eval + comparison).\nLoads pre-computed C4 profiles from c4_profiling_results.json.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nimport torch\n\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\"\nos.environ[\"HF_DATASETS_CACHE\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets\"\nos.environ[\"HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\nfrom solver import solve_mckp_constrained, uniform_quantization_baseline, enforce_monotonicity\nfrom eval_ppl import patch_attention, restore_attention, evaluate_perplexity\n\nMODEL = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-7B\"\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nWT2_REF_FILE = \"budget_sweep_results.json\"\nBUDGET = 0.25\nMAX_2BIT = 8\n\n\ndef load_wt2_reference():\n    with open(WT2_REF_FILE) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    for r in data[\"results\"]:\n        if abs(r[\"budget\"] - BUDGET) < 1e-6:\n            return r\n    raise ValueError(f\"No budget={BUDGET} entry in {WT2_REF_FILE}\")\n\n\ndef compute_agreement(alloc_a, alloc_b):\n    keys = set(alloc_a.keys()) & set(alloc_b.keys())\n    if not keys:\n        return 0.0, {}\n    agree = 0\n    disagree_details = {}\n    for k in sorted(keys):\n        a_type, a_param = alloc_a[k]\n        b_type, b_param = alloc_b[k]\n        if a_type == b_type and a_param == b_param:\n            agree += 1\n        else:\n            disagree_details[k] = {\"wt2\": [a_type, a_param], \"c4\": [b_type, b_param]}\n    return agree / len(keys), disagree_details\n\n\ndef main():\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # Load pre-computed C4 profiles\n    print(\"Loading C4 profiling results...\")\n    with open(\"c4_profiling_results.json\") as f:\n        profiling_data = json.load(f)\n    raw_profiles = profiling_data[\"profiles\"]\n    baseline_cal_ppl = profiling_data[\"baseline_calibration_ppl\"]\n    profile_time = profiling_data[\"profile_time_seconds\"]\n    print(f\"C4 baseline calibration PPL: {baseline_cal_ppl:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Profiling took {profile_time:.1f}s (pre-computed)\")\n\n    # === Phase 2: Enforce monotonicity + Solve MCKP ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"[Phase 2] Solving constrained MCKP (budget=0.25, max_2bit=8)...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    profiles = enforce_monotonicity(raw_profiles)\n    mckp_sol = solve_mckp_constrained(\n        profiles, BUDGET, mode=\"quant_only\",\n        max_2bit=MAX_2BIT, l0_exclude_2bit=True\n    )\n    uniform_sol = uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4)\n\n    print(f\"MCKP bit distribution: {mckp_sol['bit_distribution']}\")\n    print(f\"MCKP memory ratio: {mckp_sol['actual_memory_ratio']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"MCKP total distortion: {mckp_sol['total_distortion']:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"MCKP n_2bit: {mckp_sol['n_2bit']}\")\n\n    # === Phase 3: WikiText-2 PPL Eval ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"[Phase 3] WikiText-2 PPL evaluation...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    print(\"Loading model...\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\"eager\"\n    )\n    model = model.to(DEVICE)\n    model.eval()\n\n    t1 = time.time()\n\n    print(\"\\n  Evaluating FP16 baseline...\")\n    fp16_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\n    print(f\"  >>> FP16 PPL = {fp16_ppl:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n  Evaluating C4-calibrated MCKP allocation...\")\n    orig = patch_attention(model, mckp_sol[\"allocation\"])\n    c4_mckp_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\n    restore_attention(model, orig)\n    print(f\"  >>> C4-MCKP PPL = {c4_mckp_ppl:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n  Evaluating uniform 4-bit...\")\n    orig = patch_attention(model, uniform_sol[\"allocation\"])\n    uniform_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE)\n    restore_attention(model, orig)\n    print(f\"  >>> Uniform 4-bit PPL = {uniform_ppl:.4f}\")\n\n    eval_time = time.time() - t1\n\n    # === Phase 4: Cross-dataset comparison ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"[Phase 4] Cross-dataset comparison...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    wt2_ref = load_wt2_reference()\n    wt2_mckp_ppl = wt2_ref[\"mckp_ppl\"]\n    wt2_uniform_ppl = wt2_ref[\"uniform_ppl\"]\n    wt2_alloc = wt2_ref[\"mckp_allocation\"]\n    wt2_bit_dist = wt2_ref[\"bit_distribution\"]\n\n    agreement_rate, disagree_details = compute_agreement(wt2_alloc, mckp_sol[\"allocation\"])\n\n    c4_delta = uniform_ppl - c4_mckp_ppl\n    wt2_delta = wt2_uniform_ppl - wt2_mckp_ppl\n\n    total_time = time.time() - t_start\n\n    # === Print summary ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 70}\")\n    print(\"CROSS-DATASET COMPARISON RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(f\"\\n{'Metric':<40} {'WikiText-2 cal':>15} {'C4 cal':>15}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 70)\n    print(f\"{'Calibration baseline PPL':<40} {'N/A':>15} {baseline_cal_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'FP16 WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {fp16_ppl:>15.4f} {fp16_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'MCKP WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {wt2_mckp_ppl:>15.4f} {c4_mckp_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'Uniform 4-bit WikiText-2 PPL':<40} {wt2_uniform_ppl:>15.4f} {uniform_ppl:>15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'Delta (uniform - MCKP)':<40} {wt2_delta:>+15.4f} {c4_delta:>+15.4f}\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Bit distribution (WT2 cal):':<40} {wt2_bit_dist}\")\n    print(f\"{'Bit distribution (C4 cal):':<40} {mckp_sol['bit_distribution']}\")\n    print(f\"\\nAllocation agreement rate: {agreement_rate:.1%} ({int(agreement_rate * 112)}/112 heads)\")\n    print(f\"Number of disagreements: {len(disagree_details)}\")\n\n    if disagree_details:\n        print(\"\\nDisagreement details (first 20):\")\n        for i, (k, v) in enumerate(sorted(disagree_details.items())):\n            if i >= 20:\n                print(f\"  ... and {len(disagree_details) - 20} more\")\n                break\n            print(f\"  {k}: WT2={v['wt2']}, C4={v['c4']}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nProfile time: {profile_time:.1f}s, Eval time: {eval_time:.1f}s, Total: {total_time:.1f}s\")\n\n    # === Save results ===\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"c4_cross_dataset_eval\",\n        \"calibration_source\": \"c4\",\n        \"budget\": BUDGET,\n        \"max_2bit\": MAX_2BIT,\n        \"c4_calibration_baseline_ppl\": baseline_cal_ppl,\n        \"fp16_wikitext2_ppl\": fp16_ppl,\n        \"c4_mckp_wikitext2_ppl\": c4_mckp_ppl,\n        \"uniform4bit_wikitext2_ppl\": uniform_ppl,\n        \"c4_mckp_delta_over_uniform\": c4_delta,\n        \"c4_mckp_bit_distribution\": mckp_sol[\"bit_distribution\"],\n        \"c4_mckp_memory_ratio\": mckp_sol[\"actual_memory_ratio\"],\n        \"c4_mckp_total_distortion\": mckp_sol[\"total_distortion\"],\n        \"c4_mckp_allocation\": mckp_sol[\"allocation\"],\n        \"wt2_reference\": {\n            \"mckp_ppl\": wt2_mckp_ppl,\n            \"uniform_ppl\": wt2_uniform_ppl,\n            \"delta\": wt2_delta,\n            \"bit_distribution\": wt2_bit_dist,\n        },\n        \"cross_dataset_comparison\": {\n            \"allocation_agreement_rate\": agreement_rate,\n            \"n_agree\": int(agreement_rate * 112),\n            \"n_disagree\": len(disagree_details),\n            \"disagree_details\": disagree_details,\n            \"ppl_gap_mckp\": c4_mckp_ppl - wt2_mckp_ppl,\n            \"delta_gap\": c4_delta - wt2_delta,\n        },\n        \"timing\": {\n            \"profile_seconds\": profile_time,\n            \"eval_seconds\": eval_time,\n            \"total_seconds\": total_time,\n        },\n        \"c4_profiles\": raw_profiles,\n    }\n\n    with open(\"c4_eval_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"\\nSaved c4_eval_results.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF",
  "account": "root"
}

### Assistant

_source line 238_

C4 Cross-Dataset Evaluation 完成。结果汇总：

| Metric | WikiText-2 cal | C4 cal |
|--------|---------------|--------|
| MCKP PPL (WikiText-2 eval) | 11.2312 | 11.3285 |
| Uniform 4-bit PPL | 15.4454 | 15.4454 |
| Delta (uniform - MCKP) | +4.2142 | +4.1170 |
| Bit distribution | {8:4, 4:100, 2:8} | {8:4, 4:100, 2:8} |

**跨数据集一致性：**
- **Allocation agreement: 98.2%** (110/112 heads 完全一致)
- 仅 2 处差异：L3H0 (WT2:4bit↔C4:2bit) 和 L4H2 (WT2:2bit↔C4:4bit) — 本质是两个 head 互换了 2/4 bit 分配
- Bit distribution 宏观完全一致
- PPL 改善幅度差距仅 0.097（4.21 vs 4.12）

结论：MCKP 分配方案对校准数据集高度稳定，跨 WikiText-2/C4 的一致性极强。
